(41)

\displaystyle{\cal E}^{i}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{i}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{i}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{i}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{i}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}B_{a}^{j}{\cal P}^{a}_{k}\,,

(42)

\displaystyle{\cal B}_{ik}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!B_{b}^{j}\delta_{j(i}{\cal P}^{b}_{k)}\,,

(43)

and the new momenta

\displaystyle v_{i}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-\frac{1}{2}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{n}}}_{{{i}{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{n}}}_{{{i}{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{n}}}_{{{i}{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{n}}}_{{{i}{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\left({\cal P}^{-1}\right)^{m}_{b}{\cal K}^{b}_{n}\,,

(44)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{K}_{ij}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\left({\cal P}^{-1}\right)^{m}_{b}\delta_{m(i}{\cal K}^{b}_{j)}\,,

(45)

where
\left({\cal P}^{-1}\right)^{i}_{a}
is the inverse of
{\cal P}^{a}_{i}
.

The fermionic contribution to the symplectic term is given by

\displaystyle\int{\rm d}^{4}x\left[P_{\Psi{\dot{A}}}\dot{\Psi}^{\dot{A}}+\dot{\overline{\Psi}}_{\dot{A}}\overline{P_{\Psi}}{}^{\dot{A}}\right]\,,

(46)

with momenta

\displaystyle P_{\Psi}=-\overline{\Xi\Psi}\quad,\quad\overline{P_{\Psi}}=-\Xi\Psi\,,

(47)

where

\displaystyle\Xi\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{i}{2}|e|e^{t}_{I}\left(1-i\alpha\gamma^{5}\right)\gamma^{I}

(48)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{i}{2}\gamma\sqrt{\det q}\left(1-i\alpha\gamma^{5}\right)\left(\gamma^{0}-v_{i}\gamma^{i}\right)\,.

Being Grassmanian-valued, the fermionic variables obey the basic (anti-)Poisson brackets

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\!\{\Psi^{\dot{A}}(x),P_{\Psi{\dot{B}}}(y)\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\{P_{\Psi{\dot{B}}}(x),\Psi^{\dot{A}}(y)\}=\delta^{\dot{A}}_{\dot{B}}\delta^{3}(x-y)

(49)

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\!\{\overline{\Psi}_{\dot{A}}(x),\overline{P_{\Psi}}{}^{\dot{B}}(y)\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\{\overline{P_{\Psi}}{}^{\dot{B}}(x),\overline{\Psi}_{\dot{A}}(y)\}=-\delta_{\dot{A}}^{\dot{B}}\delta^{3}(x-y),

with all other combinations vanishing.

The full Poisson bracket between any two phase-space functionals
{\cal O}
and
{\cal U}
is given by

\displaystyle\{{\cal O},{\cal U}\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\int{\rm d}^{3}z\Bigg[\frac{\delta{\cal O}}{\delta A^{k}_{c}(z)}\frac{\delta{\cal U}}{\delta{\cal P}_{k}^{c}(z)}-\frac{\delta{\cal O}}{\delta{\cal P}^{c}_{k}(z)}\frac{\delta{\cal U}}{\delta A_{c}^{k}(z)}+\frac{\delta{\cal O}}{\delta B_{c}^{k}(z)}\frac{\delta{\cal U}}{\delta{\cal K}^{c}_{k}(z)}-\frac{\delta{\cal O}}{\delta{\cal K}^{c}_{k}(z)}\frac{\delta{\cal U}}{\delta B_{c}^{k}(z)}

(50)

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad\qquad+\frac{\delta^{R}{\cal O}}{\delta\Psi^{\dot{A}}(z)}\frac{\delta{\cal U}}{\delta P_{\Psi{\dot{A}}}(z)}+\frac{\delta^{R}{\cal O}}{\delta P_{\Psi{\dot{A}}}(z)}\frac{\delta{\cal U}}{\delta\Psi^{\dot{A}}(z)}-\frac{\delta^{R}{\cal O}}{\delta\overline{\Psi}_{\dot{A}}(z)}\frac{\delta{\cal U}}{\delta\overline{P_{\Psi}}{}^{\dot{A}}(z)}-\frac{\delta^{R}{\cal O}}{\delta\overline{P_{\Psi}}{}^{\dot{A}}(z)}\frac{\delta{\cal U}}{\delta\overline{\Psi}_{\dot{A}}(z)}\Bigg]

—where the superscript
R
denotes a right derivative and left derivatives are always used otherwise—or a variation using other canonical phase-space coordinates.

The symplectic structure defined by the Poisson bracket (
50
) corresponds to an extended phase space whose number of canonical pairs exceeds that of the dynamical fields implied by the action.
Therefore, in order to describe the same theory of the Lagrangian formulation, a phase-space reduction must take place by imposing a specific set of constraints that we identify in the next subsection.

III.3
Non-dynamical variables and phase-space reduction

The 6 components
\omega_{t}^{IJ}
are non-dynamical because their time derivatives do not appear in the action and imply 6 first-class constraints.
In the following, we will denote these components by

K_{t}^{i}=\omega_{t}^{0i}\quad,\quad\Gamma_{t}^{i}=\frac{1}{2}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{l}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{l}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{l}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{l}}}}\omega^{kl}_{t}\,.

(51)

Similarly, no time derivatives of the lapse and the shift appear in the action, leading to 4 additional first-class constraints.
The first-class status of these constraints is shown in the next section.

Furthermore, the relation

{\cal K}^{a}_{i}=\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\cal P}^{a}_{j}v_{k}\,,

(52)

derived from comparing (
37
) and (
38
), implies that the momentum components
\mathfrak{K}_{ij}
vanish on physical solutions,

\mathfrak{K}_{ij}=0\,,

(53)

constituting 6 second-class constraints on the phase space.
This implies that the time derivatives of the components
{\cal B}_{ij}
drop out from the symplectic term (
33
), and hence constitute 6 additional non-dynamical components of the connection, which lead to another 6 second-class constraints

\frac{\delta{\cal H}}{\delta{\cal B}^{ij}}=0\,,

(54)

where
{\cal H}
is the Hamiltonian.
This constitutes a secondary second-class constraint enforcing the preservation of (
53
) under time evolution:
\delta{\cal H}/\delta{\cal B}^{ij}=-\{\mathfrak{K}_{ij},{\cal H}\}=0
.
The second-class status of these constraints and the fact that no tertiary constraints are necessary to enforce the preservation of (
54
) under time evolution are shown in Section
V
.

The resulting 10 first-class constraints, together with the total of 12 second-class constraints, reduce the 18 gravitational canonical pairs to only 2 degrees of freedom on physical solutions, in agreement with our counting in the Lagrangian formulation.

On the other hand, the symplectic term (
46
) implies that the configuration variables
\Psi
and
\overline{\Psi}
are kinematically independent fields, which in turn imply 16 canonical pairs of the fermion field.
However, imposing the reality conditions

\overline{\Psi}=\Psi^{\dagger}\beta\quad,\quad\overline{P_{\Psi}}=\beta P_{\Psi}^{\dagger}\,,

(55)

lowers the total fermionic degrees of freedom from 16 to only 8 on dynamical solutions—the reality conditions can be consistently imposed if all the constraints are Hermitian, a property that will be applied in the forthcoming canonical decomposition and hence will always be assumed to hold.
If the Hamiltonian is a fermion bilinear with up to first-order derivatives of the fermionic variables—which is the case in our system as shown in the next section—then Hamilton’s equations of motion for
\Psi
and
P_{\Psi}
result in first-order differential equations of
\Psi
and
P_{\Psi}
,

O\Psi=0

(56)

and

P_{\Psi}U=0\,,

(57)

with some linear operators
O
and
U
, respectively.
This implies that the 8 components of
\Psi
reduce to 4 degrees of freedom but so will the components of
P_{\Psi}
, totaling 8 fermionic degrees of freedom, rather than the 4 implied by the action.
The reason for the doubling of the fermionic degrees of freedom can be identified as the fact that the momentum
P_{\Psi}
is not related to
\overline{\Psi}
by the relation (
47
)—and their conjugate versions—at the kinematical level.
These relations can hold on dynamical solutions, but only as a special case where they are restricted to satisfy the relation (
47
) and its conjugated version because they do not arise from the solution to the linear equations of motion alone: This requires the imposition of 8 additional constraints, given by

C_{\overline{\Psi}}=P_{\Psi}+\overline{\Psi}\,\overline{\Xi}

(58)

and

C_{\Psi}=\overline{P_{\Psi}}+\Xi\Psi\,,

(59)

which, depending on the details of the first-class constraints, may be second class.
We reiterate that the imposition of these constraints is different from that of the reality conditions (
55
) and must be implemented in addition to the latter to describe Dirac fermions.

If the constraints (
58
) and (
59
) are not imposed, then the canonical system implies a more general theory with 4 additional fermionic degrees of freedom compared to the Dirac theory that still satisfies the reality conditions.
Such a theory is instead based on a different action contribution, given by

\displaystyle S_{F}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\int{\rm d}^{4}x|e|\frac{i}{2}e^{\mu}_{I}\Big[\overline{\Phi}\gamma^{I}\left(1-i\alpha\gamma^{5}\right)D_{\mu}\Psi

(60)

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-D_{\mu}\overline{\Psi}\left(1-i\alpha\gamma^{5}\right)\gamma^{I}\Phi\Big]\,,

where
\Phi
is an independent fermion field.
The fermionic equations of motion of this action,
\delta S_{F}/\delta\overline{\Phi}=0
and
\delta S_{F}/\delta\overline{\Psi}=0
, are respectively given by

|e|\frac{i}{2}e^{\mu}_{I}\gamma^{I}\left(1-i\alpha\gamma^{5}\right)D_{\mu}\Psi=0

(61)

and

\displaystyle\!\!|e|\frac{i}{2}e^{\mu}_{I}\left(1-i\alpha\gamma^{5}\right)\gamma^{I}D_{\mu}\Phi

(62)

\displaystyle\!\!\quad+\frac{i}{2}D_{\mu}\left(|e|e^{\mu}_{I}\right)\left(1-i\alpha\gamma^{5}\right)\gamma^{I}\Phi=0\,,

which differ from (
27
) and the solutions are independent of
\alpha
, up to its possible appearance in
D_{\mu}\left(|e|e^{\mu}_{I}\right)
.
The covariant divergence of the tetrad based on this extended action is richer than that of the Dirac action:

\displaystyle D_{\mu}\left(|e|e^{\mu}_{I}\right)\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{8\pi G}{4}\frac{|e|}{1+\zeta^{2}}\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{\zeta}{2}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.08473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.08473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47124pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.19374pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{R}{S}}}_{{{I}{N}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.28708pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.3479pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.08473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.08473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47124pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.19374pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{R}{S}}}_{{{I}{N}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.28708pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.3479pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.08473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.08473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47124pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.19374pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{R}{S}}}_{{{I}{N}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.28708pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.3479pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.08473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.08473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47124pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.19374pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{R}{S}}}_{{{I}{N}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.28708pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.3479pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\overline{\Phi}\left(1+i\alpha\gamma^{5}\right)[\gamma^{N},S_{RS}]_{+}\Psi+\overline{\Phi}\left(1+i\alpha\gamma^{5}\right)[\gamma^{N},S_{IN}]\Psi+c.c.\right)

(63)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-\frac{3}{8}8\pi G\frac{|e|}{1+\zeta^{2}}\left(\overline{\Phi}\gamma_{I}\left[\left(\zeta-\alpha\right)\gamma^{5}-i\left(1+\alpha\zeta\right)\right]\Psi+c.c.\right)\,.

The symplectic term of the action contribution (
60
) is given by (
46
) too, but with the momentum

P_{\Psi}=-\overline{\Xi\Phi}\,.

(64)

Notice, however, that, imposing
\Phi=\Psi
a posteriori, equations (
61
) and (
62
) would imply that
D_{\mu}\left(|e|e^{\mu}_{I}\right)
vanishes; however, according to (
63
),
D_{\mu}\left(|e|e^{\mu}_{I}\right)
is generally non-vanishing with the exceptions lying on a specific small set of configurations of the fermionic fields.
Solutions admitting
\Phi=\Psi
are therefore extremely limited.
Hence, the implementation of the Dirac-fermion constraints (
58
) and (
59
) must be performed a priori as a phase-space reduction rather than as a restriction to the solutions to the equations of motion of the extended phase space to recover a Dirac-fermion system—this is analogous to a free two-dimensional single-particle system with its motion confined to a ring: Circular motion cannot be recovered by restricting the solutions (linear motion) of the free single-particle system; the phase-space reduction must be performed prior to deriving the equations of motion from the Lagrangian or, alternatively, by using Lagrange multipliers to constrain the motion.
This can be achieved in the canonical formulation by use of Dirac brackets, following the treatment of second-class constraints.

We conclude that the 34 canonical pairs of the extended phase space described by (
50
) reduce to only 6 degrees of freedom (2 of which are gravitational and 4 are fermionic) upon the imposition of the reality conditions (
55
), the Dirac-fermion constraints (
58
) and (
59
), and the gravitational second-class constraints (
53
) and (
54
), as well as the first-class constraints (and use of the equations of motion they generate) discussed in the next section.
The implementation of the second-class constraints via Dirac brackets is discussed in Section
V
.

III.4
Canonical decomposition of geometric quantities

Having identified the canonical phase-space variables in terms of the Lagrangian fields, it is useful to invert these relations for a systematic canonical decomposition of the action.
We gather several of these relations in this subsection.

The inverse spatial metric is given by

\displaystyle q^{ab}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{|\det{\cal P}|^{-1}}{8\pi G}\left(\delta^{ij}+\gamma^{2}v^{i}v^{j}\right){\cal P}^{a}_{i}{\cal P}^{b}_{j}

(65)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{\gamma^{2}|\det{\cal P}|^{-1}}{8\pi G}\delta^{ij}\left({\cal P}^{a}_{i}{\cal P}^{b}_{j}-{\cal K}^{a}_{i}{\cal K}^{b}_{j}\right)\bigg|_{\mathfrak{K}=0}\,.

The determinant of
q_{ab}
is then given by

\det q=(8\pi G)^{3}\frac{|\det{\cal P}|}{\gamma^{2}}\,.

(66)

In terms of the new canonical variables, the covariant derivative of the fermion field is given by

\displaystyle D_{t}\Psi\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\dot{\Psi}-iK_{t}^{i}S_{0i}\Psi-i\Gamma_{t}^{i}S_{i}\Psi\,,

(67)

\displaystyle D_{a}\Psi\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\partial_{a}\Psi-iK^{i}_{a}S_{0i}\Psi-i\Gamma^{i}_{a}S_{i}\Psi\,,

(68)

where we defined

S^{i}=\frac{1}{2}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}S^{jk}\,.

(69)

The identity

\left(\gamma^{0}-v_{i}\gamma^{i}\right)\left(\gamma^{0}-v_{j}\gamma^{j}\right)=\frac{1}{\gamma^{2}}

(70)

is useful to invert (
47
) into

\displaystyle\overline{\Psi}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-P_{\Psi}\overline{\Xi^{-1}}\,,

(71)

\displaystyle\Psi\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-\Xi^{-1}\overline{P_{\Psi}}\,,

(72)

where

\Xi^{-1}=-\frac{2i}{1+\alpha^{2}}\frac{\gamma}{\sqrt{\det q}}\left(\gamma^{0}-v_{i}\gamma^{i}\right)\left(1+i\alpha\gamma^{5}\right)

(73)

is the inverse of
\Xi
.

It is useful to write the Dirac equation (
27
) in terms of the canonical variables because it serves as a guide to construct the Hamiltonian that can generate it:

\displaystyle\dot{\Psi}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\Theta^{a}\partial_{a}\Psi+i\Omega\Psi

(74)

\displaystyle\!\!-\frac{\gamma^{2}\left(\gamma^{0}-v_{i}\gamma^{i}\right)\left(1-i\alpha\gamma^{5}\right)D_{\mu}\left(|e|e^{\mu}_{I}\gamma^{I}\right)\Psi}{2(8\pi G)^{3/2}\sqrt{|\det{\cal P}|}}\,,

where

\Theta^{a}=N^{a}+\frac{8\pi GN}{\sqrt{\det q}}{\cal P}^{a}_{q}\left[v^{q}-\left(\gamma^{0}-v_{k}\gamma^{k}\right)\gamma^{q}\right]\,,

(75)

\displaystyle\!\!\Omega\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-\left[K_{t}^{i}-\left(N^{a}+N\frac{8\pi G}{\sqrt{\det q}}v^{q}{\cal P}^{a}_{q}\right)K_{a}^{i}\right]S_{0i}

(76)

\displaystyle\!\!-\left[\Gamma_{t}^{i}-\left(N^{a}+N\frac{8\pi G}{\sqrt{\det q}}v^{q}{\cal P}^{a}_{q}\right)\Gamma_{a}^{i}\right]S_{i}

\displaystyle\!\!-N\frac{8\pi G}{\sqrt{\det q}}\left(\gamma^{0}-v_{m}\gamma^{m}\right){\cal P}^{a}_{j}\gamma^{j}\left(K_{a}^{i}S_{0i}+\Gamma_{a}^{i}S_{i}\right)\,,

and

\displaystyle D_{\mu}\left(|e|e^{\mu}_{I}\gamma^{I}\right)\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{2}{i}\frac{1+i\alpha\gamma^{5}}{1+\alpha^{2}}\Big[\{\Xi,{\cal H}\}-\partial_{a}\left(\overline{\Theta^{a}}\Xi\right)

(77)

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad\qquad\quad-i\left(\overline{\Omega}\Xi-\Xi\Omega\right)\Big]\,,

where

\displaystyle\overline{\Omega}\Xi-\Xi\Omega\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{i}{2}\gamma\sqrt{\det q}\left(1+i\alpha\gamma^{5}\right)\Bigg[-i\left[K_{t}^{i}-\left(N^{a}+N\frac{8\pi G}{\sqrt{\det q}}v^{q}{\cal P}^{a}_{q}\right)K_{a}^{i}\right]\left(\gamma_{i}-v_{i}\gamma^{0}\right)

(78)

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad\qquad\qquad+i\left[\Gamma_{t}^{i}-\left(N^{a}+N\frac{8\pi G}{\sqrt{\det q}}v^{q}{\cal P}^{a}_{q}\right)\Gamma_{a}^{i}\right]v_{m}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{l}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{l}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{l}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{l}}}}\gamma^{l}-i\frac{8\pi GN}{\gamma^{2}\sqrt{\det q}}{\cal P}^{a}_{j}\left(K_{a}^{j}\gamma^{0}+\Gamma_{a}^{i}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{l}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{l}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{l}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{l}}}}\gamma^{l}\right)\Bigg]\,.

The bracket
\{\Xi,{\cal H}\}
cannot be written definitively in terms of the phase-space variables at this stage because it requires knowledge of the Hamiltonian, yet to be computed.
However, on shell, it must be such that Eq. (
77
) coincides with (
26
) if the Dirac-fermion constraints are imposed.

Using the above results, the equation of motion for the fermionic momentum is given by

\displaystyle\dot{P}_{\Psi}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\partial_{a}\left(P_{\Psi}\Theta^{a}\right)-iP_{\Psi}\Omega

(79)

\displaystyle\!\!+\frac{\gamma P_{\Psi}\left(1-i\alpha\gamma^{5}\right)\left(\gamma^{0}-v_{n}\gamma^{n}\right)D_{\mu}\left(|e|e^{\mu}_{I}\gamma^{I}\right)}{\sqrt{\det q}}\,.

Finally, relevant components of the torsion equation (
23
) for the forthcoming canonical analysis can be expressed by the symmetric internal tensor

\displaystyle{\cal T}^{ij}\!\!

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\!\!-\frac{\delta_{k}^{(i}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{j)}{p}{q}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.20389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.00278pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.36322pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.688pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{{j)}{p}{q}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.20389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.00278pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.36322pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.688pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{{j)}{p}{q}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.20389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.00278pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.36322pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.688pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{{j)}{p}{q}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.20389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.00278pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\t (80)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\left(\delta^{ij}\delta^{p}_{q}-\delta^{p(i}\delta_{q}^{j)}\right){\cal P}^{d}_{p}\Gamma_{d}^{q}-\delta_{q}^{(i}\delta^{j)p}{\cal K}^{d}_{p}K_{d}^{q}

\displaystyle\!\!+\delta_{k}^{(i}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{j)}{p}{q}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.20389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.00278pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.36322pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.688pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{{j)}{p}{q}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.20389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.00278pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.36322pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.688pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{{j)}{p}{q}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.20389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.00278pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.36322pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.688pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{{j)}{p}{q}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.20389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.00278pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textsty P}^{c}_{p}{\cal P}^{d}_{q}\partial_{d}\left({\cal P}^{-1}\right)_{c}^{k}

\displaystyle\!\!+\frac{1}{2}\frac{1}{1+\zeta^{2}}\left[P_{\Psi}\left(\zeta-i\gamma^{5}\right)\upsilon^{ij}\Psi+c.c.\right]\,,

where

\upsilon^{ij}=\delta^{ij}+\gamma^{2}\left(\gamma^{0}-v_{u}\gamma^{u}\right)v^{(i}\gamma^{j)}\,.

(81)

IV
First-class constraints

IV.1
Constraints

The action
S=S_{G}+S_{F}
can be written as

\displaystyle S\!\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\!\int{\rm d}^{4}x\bigg[\tilde{\cal P}^{a}_{i}F_{ta}^{0i}+\tilde{\cal K}^{a}_{i}{\cal F}_{ta}^{i}+P_{\Psi}D_{t}\Psi+D_{t}\overline{\Psi}\,\overline{P_{\Psi}}

(82)

\displaystyle\!\!\!\qquad\quad+\frac{|e|}{16\pi G}e^{a}_{I}e^{b}_{J}\mathchoice{P^{{{I}{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}{L}}}}{P^{{{I}{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}{L}}}}{P^{{{I}{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}{L}}}}{P^{{{I}{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}{L}}}}F^{KL}_{ab}

\displaystyle\!\!\!\qquad\quad+\frac{i}{2}|e|e^{a}_{I}\left(\overline{\Psi}\gamma^{I}D_{a}\Psi-D_{a}\overline{\Psi}\gamma^{I}\Psi\right)\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\!\int{\rm d}^{4}x\left[\tilde{\cal P}^{a}_{i}\dot{K}_{a}^{i}+\tilde{\cal K}^{a}_{i}\dot{\Gamma}_{a}^{i}+P_{\Psi}\dot{\Psi}+\dot{\overline{\Psi}}\,\overline{P_{\Psi}}\right]

\displaystyle\!\!\!-H[|N|]-H_{a}[N^{a}]-L_{i}[K_{t}^{i}]-G_{i}[\Gamma_{t}^{i}]\,,

which is linear in all Lagrange multipliers and, therefore, all the local expressions of
H
,
H_{a}
,
L_{i}
, and
G_{i}
are constraints.
Only the first line contributes to the symplectic term and to
L_{i}
and
G_{i}
, while only the second and third lines contribute to
H
and
H_{a}
.
Notice that the absolute value of the lapse
|N|
smears the Hamiltonian constraint; this is because the action depends on the absolute value of the co-tetrad’s determinant
|e|
.
In the following, however, we will often write
H[N]
, but it is understood that we use only its magnitude.
This detail is not relevant in most applications because the gauge choices of the lapse that change sign would require that it vanishes in some region of the spacetime, which would amount to a limitation of the coordinate chart related to that specific gauge choice and hence another well-defined gauge, with locally non-vanishing lapse, is required to describe that region of the spacetime; however, it will be important for the discussion on discrete symmetries presented in Section
VIII
.

We first focus on the Lorentz–Gauss constraints, given by
L_{i}=L_{i}^{(G)}+L_{i}^{(F)}
and
G_{i}=G_{i}^{(G)}+G_{i}^{(F)}
, respectively, where

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-\partial_{a}{\cal P}^{a}_{i}-\zeta\partial_{a}{\cal K}^{a}_{i}-\left({\cal P}^{a}_{k}B_{a}^{l}-{\cal and

G_{i}^{(F)}=-i\left[P_{\Psi}S_{i}{\Psi}-\overline{\Psi}\,S_{i}\overline{P_{\Psi}}\right]\,.

(86)

The gravitational contributions to these constraints generate proper Lorentz transformations of the triad
e^{a}_{I}
and the spatial strength tensor
F^{IJ}_{ab}
even off shell
Tetrads
.
The fermionic contributions preserve the generation of Lorentz transformations of the gravitational variables and also generate those of the fermionic variables:

\{\Psi,L_{i}[\beta^{i}]\}=-i\beta^{i}S_{0i}{\Psi}\quad,\quad\{\Psi,G_{i}[\theta^{i}]\}=-i\theta^{i}S_{i}{\Psi}\,,

(87)

\{P_{\Psi},L_{i}[\beta^{i}]\}=i\beta^{i}P_{\Psi}S_{0i}\quad,\quad\{P_{\Psi},G_{i}[\theta^{i}]\}=i\theta^{i}P_{\Psi}S_{i}\,.

(88)

The vector constraint is given by
H_{a}=H^{(G)}_{a}+H^{(F)}_{a}
, where

\displaystyle H_{a}^{(G)}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\tilde{\cal P}^{b}_{i}F^{0i}_{ab}+\tilde{\cal K}^{b}_{i}{\cal F}^{i}_{ab}

(89)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!{\cal P}^{b}_{i}\left(F^{0i}_{ab}-\zeta{\cal F}^{i}_{ab}\right)+{\cal K}^{b}_{i}\left({\cal F}^{i}_{ab}+\zeta F^{0i}_{ab}\right)\,,

is the gravitational contribution and

\displaystyle H_{a}^{(F)}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!P_{\Psi}D_{a}\Psi+\overline{D_{a}\Psi}\;\overline{P_{\Psi}}

(90)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!P_{\Psi}\partial_{a}\Psi+\partial_{a}\overline{\Psi}\;\overline{P_{\Psi}}-K_{a}^{i}L^{(F)}_{i}-\Gamma_{a}^{i}G^{(F)}_{i}\,,

is the fermionic contribution.

Above, we used the expressions

F_{ab}^{0i}=2\partial_{[a}K_{b]}^{i}-2\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}K_{[a}^{j}\Gamma_{b]}^{k}

(91)

and

\displaystyle{\cal F}^{i}_{ab}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{1}{2}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{l}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{l}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{l}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.5584 (92)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!2\partial_{[a}\Gamma_{b]}^{i}+\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyl \makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}\Gamma_{a}^{j}\Gamma_{b}^{k}\,.

The linear combinations with the Barbero–Immirzi parameter can be written more compactly in the
A,B
variables,

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!F^{0i}_{ab}-\zeta{\cal F}^{i}_{ab}=2\partial_{[a}A_{b]}^{i}

(93)

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\qquad+2\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{l}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{l}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{l}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{l}}}}\frac{1}{1+\zeta^{2}}\left(\frac{\zeta}{2}\left(A_{[a}^{k}A_{b]}^{l}-B_{[a}^{k}B_{b]}^{l}\right)-A_{[a}^{k}B_{b]}^{l}\right)\,,

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!{\cal F}^{i}_{ab}+\zeta F^{0i}_{ab}=2\partial_{[a}B_{b]}^{i}

(94)

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\qquad+2\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{l}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{l}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{l}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.558
