A_{[a}^{k}B_{b]}^{l}\right)\,.

The Hamiltonian constraint is given by

H=H^{(1)}+H^{(2)}\,,

(95)

where

H^{(1)}=H_{a}\frac{\gamma\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}v^{q}{\cal P}^{a}_{q}\,.

and
H^{(2)}=H^{(2)}_{(G)}+H^{(2)}_{(F)}
with the gravitational contribution

\displaystyle H^{(2)}_{(G)}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-\frac{\sqrt{|(\det{\cal P})^{-1}|}}{2\gamma\sqrt{8\pi G}}{\cal P}^{a}_{p}{\cal P}^{b}_{q}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{p}{q}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.36322pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.688pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{\epsilon^{{{p}{q}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.36322pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.688pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{\epsilon^{{{p}{q}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.36322pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.688pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{\epsilon^{{{p}{q}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.36322pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.688pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}\left({\cal F}^{i}_{ab}+\zeta F^{0i}_{ab}\right)

(96)

\displaystyle\!\!+\frac{\sqrt{|\det{\cal P}}|}{\gamma}\sqrt{8\pi G}\Lambda\,.

and the fermionic contribution

\displaystyle H_{(F)}^{(2)}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-\frac{\gamma\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}{\cal P}^{a}_{j}\Big[P_{\Psi}\left(\gamma^{0}-v_{m}\gamma^{m}\right)\gamma^{j}\left(\partial_{a}\Psi+i\left(K_{a}^{i}S_{0i}+\Gamma_{a}^{i}S_{i}\right)\Psi\right)

(97)

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad+\left(\partial_{a}\overline{\Psi}-i\overline{\Psi}\left(K_{a}^{i}S_{0i}+\Gamma_{a}^{i}S_{i}\right)\right)\gamma^{j}\left(\gamma^{0}-v_{m}\gamma^{m}\right)\overline{P_{\Psi}}\Big]\,.

(The various replacements of
\overline{\Psi}
for
P_{\Psi}
, and their respective conjugates, are necessary to recover the correct equations of motion and to respect the discrete symmetries of the theory as will be clear in Section
VIII
.)

Because all of the first-class constraints are independent of
\alpha
, we find that the theory is kinematically independent of the non-minimal coupling parameter.

IV.2
Constraint algebra

A straightforward computation shows that
L_{i}
and
G_{i}
satisfy the Lorentz algebra,

\displaystyle\{L_{i}[K_{t}^{i}],L_{j}[\beta^{j}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-G_{k}[\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}K_{t}^{i}\beta^{j}]\,,

(98)

\displaystyle\{G_{i}[\Gamma_{t}^{i}],L_{j}[\beta^{j}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!L_{k}[\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}\Gamma_{t}^{i}\beta^{j}]\,,

(99)

\displaystyle\{G_{i}[\Gamma_{t}^{i}],G_{j}[\theta^{j}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!G_{k}[\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}\Gamma_{t}^{i}\theta^{j}]\,.

(100)

Now consider the linear combination
\mathfrak{D}_{b}[N^{b}]=H_{a}[N^{b}]+G_{i}[\Gamma_{b}^{i}N^{b}]+L_{i}[K_{b}^{i}N^{b}]
; in its local form, it is given by

\displaystyle\mathfrak{D}_{a}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!{\cal P}^{b}_{i}\partial_{a}A_{b}^{i}+{\cal K}^{b}_{i}\partial_{a}B_{b}^{i}-\partial_{b}\left({\cal P}^{b}_{i}A_{a}^{i}+{\cal K}^{b}_{i}B_{a}^{i}\right)

(101)

\displaystyle\!\!+P_{\Psi}\partial_{a}\Psi+\partial_{a}\overline{\Psi}\,\overline{P_{\Psi}}\,.

It is then clear that the functional
\mathfrak{D}_{a}[N^{a}]
generates spatial diffeomorphisms of all phase-space variables.
It follows that

\displaystyle\{{\cal O},H_{b}[N^{b}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\mathcal{L}_{\vec{N}}{\cal O}+\{G_{i}\left[\Gamma_{b}^{i}N^{b}\right],{\cal O}\}

(102)

\displaystyle\!\!+\{L_{i}\left[K_{b}^{i}N^{b}\right],{\cal O}\}\,,

for any phase-space function
{\cal O}
.

Using the above, we obtain that the vector constraint commutes with the Lorentz–Gauss constraints,

\displaystyle\{H_{a}[N^{a}],G_{k}[\theta^{k}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,,

(103)

\displaystyle\{H_{a}[N^{a}],L_{k}[\beta^{k}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,.

(104)

Similarly, using (
102
), together with (
103
) and (
104
), we obtain

\displaystyle\{H_{a}[N^{a}],H_{c}[\epsilon^{c}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-H_{a}\left[\mathcal{L}_{\vec{\epsilon}}N^{a}\right]-G_{i}\left[\epsilon^{a}N^{b}{\cal F}^{i}_{ab}\right]

(105)

\displaystyle\!\!-L_{i}\left[\epsilon^{a}N^{b}F^{0i}_{ab}\right]\,.

The second and third lines of the action (
82
) are Lorentz invariant and they equal
-H[N]-H_{a}[N^{a}]
.
Using (
103
) and (
104
), as well as the fact that the Lorentz–Gauss constraints generate proper Lorentz transformations on and off shell, it follows that the Lorentz–Gauss constraints commute with the Hamiltonian constraint too:

\displaystyle\{H[N],G_{k}[\theta^{k}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,,

(106)

\displaystyle\{H[N],L_{k}[\beta^{k}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,.

(107)

Using (
102
), we obtain the bracket

\displaystyle\{H[N],H_{c}[\epsilon^{c}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-H[\mathcal{L}_{\vec{\epsilon}}N]+G_{i}\left[N\epsilon^{a}{\cal F}^{i}_{\bar{0}a}\right]

(108)

\displaystyle\!\!+L_{i}\left[N\epsilon^{a}F^{0i}_{\bar{0}a}\right]\,,

where we defined the expressions

\displaystyle F_{\bar{0}a}^{0i}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{1}{N}\{K_{a}^{i},H[N]\}\,,

\displaystyle{\cal F}_{\bar{0}a}^{i}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{1}{N}\{\Gamma_{a}^{i},H[N]\}\,,

(109)

which are compatible with the identification of the normal components of the strength tensor,

\displaystyle n^{\mu}F^{0i}_{\mu a}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{1}{N}F_{ta}^{0i}-\frac{N^{b}}{N}F_{ba}^{0i}=\frac{1}{N}\{K_{a}^{i},H[N]\}\,,

\displaystyle n^{\mu}{\cal F}_{\mu a}^{i}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{1}{N}{\cal F}_{ta}^{i}-\frac{N^{b}}{N}{\cal F}_{ba}^{i}=\frac{1}{N}\{\Gamma_{a}^{i},H[N]\}\,.

(110)

Using all the above, we compute the bracket of the Hamiltonian constraint with itself in steps.
First, using (
105
) and (
102
), we obtain

\{H^{(1)}[N],H^{(1)}[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}=0\,,

(111)

and

\displaystyle\!\!\{H^{(2)}[N],H^{(1)}[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}+\{H^{(1)}[N],H^{(2)}[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}

(112)

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad=-H^{(2)}\left[\frac{\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}\gamma v^{q}{\cal P}^{a}_{q}(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\partial_{a}N-N\partial_{a}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})\right]

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad\quad-H_{a}\left[q^{ab}\left(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\partial_{b}N-N\partial_{b}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\right)\right]\,,

where
q^{ab}
is the inverse spatial metric (
65
).
Using the antisymmetry of the Poisson brackets—with proper care of the sign changes from commuting the fermionic variables—it follows that

\displaystyle\!\!\{H^{(2)}_{(G)}[N],H^{(2)}_{(G)}[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}

(113)

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad=H^{(2)}_{(G)}\left[\frac{\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}\gamma v^{q}{\cal P}^{b}_{q}\left(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\partial_{b}N-N\partial_{b}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\right)\right]\,,

\{H^{(2)}_{(F)}[N],H^{(2)}_{(F)}[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}=0\,,

(114)

and

\displaystyle\!\!\{H^{(2)}_{(G)}[N],H^{(2)}_{(F)}[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}+\{H^{(2)}_{(F)}[N],H^{(2)}_{(G)}[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}

(115)

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad=H^{(2)}_{(F)}\left[\frac{\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}\gamma v^{q}{\cal P}^{b}_{q}\left(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\partial_{b}N-N\partial_{b}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\right)\right]\,.

Therefore, the full bracket of the Hamiltonian constraint with itself is given by

\{H[N],H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}=-H_{a}\left[q^{ab}\left(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\partial_{b}N-N\partial_{b}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\right)\right]\,.

(116)

In summary, the constraint algebra in the extended phase space is given by

\displaystyle\{H_{a}[N^{a}],H_{c}[\epsilon^{c}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-H_{a}\left[\mathcal{L}_{\vec{\epsilon}}N^{a}\right]-G_{i}\left[\epsilon^{a}N^{b}{\cal F}^{i}_{ab}\right]

(117)

\displaystyle\!\!-L_{i}\left[\epsilon^{a}N^{b}F^{0i}_{ab}\right]\,,

\displaystyle\{H[N],H_{c}[\epsilon^{c}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-H[\mathcal{L}_{\vec{\epsilon}}N]+G_{i}\left[N\epsilon^{a}{\cal F}^{i}_{\bar{0}a}\right]

(118)

\displaystyle\!\!+L_{i}\left[N\epsilon^{a}F^{0i}_{\bar{0}a}\right]\,,

\displaystyle\{H[N],H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-H_{a}\left[q^{ab}\left(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\partial_{b}N-N\partial_{b}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\right)\right],\qquad

(119)

\displaystyle\{H_{c}[N^{c}],G_{k}[\theta^{k}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,,

(120)

\displaystyle\{H_{c}[N^{c}],L_{k}[\beta^{k}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,,

(121)

\displaystyle\{H[N],G_{k}[\theta^{k}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,,

(122)

\displaystyle\{H[N],L_{k}[\beta^{k}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,,

(123)

\displaystyle\{G_{i}[\Gamma_{t}^{i}],G_{j}[\theta^{j}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!G_{k}\left[\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}\Gamma_{t}^{i}\theta^{j}\right]\,,

(124)

\displaystyle\{G_{i}[\Gamma_{t}^{i}],L_{j}[\beta^{j}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!L_{k}\left[\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}\Gamma_{t}^{i}\beta^{j}\right]\,,

(125)

\displaystyle\{L_{i}[K_{t}^{i}],L_{j}[\beta^{j}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-G_{k}\left[\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}K_{t}^{i}\beta^{j}\right]\,,

(126)

which is identical to that of the vacuum system
Tetrads
.

IV.3
Gauge transformations and covariance

While the transformation of phase-space functions under the flow of the first-class constraints is directly provided by the action of Poisson brackets, this is not the case for the non-dynamical fields
N
,
N^{a}
,
K_{t}^{i}
, and
\Gamma_{t}^{i}
.
Instead, their transformations are defined by the preservation of the form of Hamilton’s equations of motion along this flow—this is the definition of a gauge transformation—and hence are determined by the constraint algebra (
117
)-(
126
)
pons1997gauge
;
salisbury1983realization
;
Tetrads
:

\displaystyle\delta_{\epsilon,\theta,\beta}N\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\dot{\epsilon}^{0}+\epsilon^{b}\partial_{b}N-N^{b}\partial_{b}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\,,

(127)

\displaystyle\delta_{\epsilon,\theta,\beta}N^{a}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\dot{\epsilon}^{a}+\epsilon^{b}\partial_{b}N^{a}-N^{b}\partial_{b}\epsilon^{a}

(128)

\displaystyle\!\!+q^{ab}\left(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\partial_{b}N-N\partial_{b}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\right)\,,

\displaystyle\delta_{\epsilon,\theta,\beta}K_{t}^{i}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\dot{\beta}^{i}+\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}\left(\beta^{j}\Gamma_{t}^{k}+\theta^{j}K_{t}^{k}\right)+\epsilon^{a}N^{b}F^{0i}_{ab}

(129)

\displaystyle\!\!+\left(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}N^{a}-N\epsilon^{a}\right)F^{0i}_{\bar{0}a}\,,

\displaystyle\delta_{\epsilon,\theta,\beta}\Gamma_{t}^{i}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\dot{\theta}^{i}+\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}\left(\theta^{j}\Gamma_{t}^{k}-\beta^{j}K_{t}^{k}\right)+\epsilon^{a}N^{b}{\cal F}^{i}_{ab}

(130)

\displaystyle\!\!+\left(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}N^{a}-N\epsilon^{a}\right){\cal F}^{i}_{\bar{0}a}\,.

That is, the canonical gauge transformations are given by the combined operation

\displaystyle{\cal O}\!\!

\displaystyle\to

\displaystyle\!\!{\cal O}+\{{\cal O},H[\epsilon^{0}]+H_{a}[\epsilon^{a}]+G_{i}[\theta^{i}]+L_{i}[\beta^{i}]\}\,,

\displaystyle N\!\!

\displaystyle\to

\displaystyle\!\!N+\delta_{\epsilon,\theta,\beta}N\quad,\quad N^{a}\to N^{a}+\delta_{\epsilon,\theta,\beta}N^{a}\,,

\displaystyle K_{t}^{i}\!\!

\displaystyle\to

\displaystyle\!\!K_{t}^{i}+\delta_{\epsilon,\theta,\beta}K_{t}^{i}\quad,\quad\Gamma_{t}^{i}\to\Gamma_{t}^{i}+\delta_{\epsilon,\theta,\beta}\Gamma_{t}^{i}\,,

(131)

for all phase-space functions
{\cal O}
.

The transformations of the non-dynamical fields are essential to understand the transformations of the physical fields which generally depend on them—for instance, the components of the spacetime metric (
28
) depend on
N
and
N^{a}
, while the connection, and hence also the strength and torsion tensor fields, depend on
K_{t}^{i}
and
\Gamma_{t}^{i}
.

The canonical system is covariant only if the canonical gauge transformations of the physical fields correspond to spacetime diffeomorphisms and infinitesimal SL(2,
\mathbb{C}
) transformations on shell.
For instance, the canonical gauge transformation of the spacetime metric must satisfy the covariance condition

\delta_{\epsilon,\theta,\beta}g_{\mu\nu}\big|_{\rm OS}=\mathcal{L}_{\xi}g_{\mu\nu}\big|_{\rm OS}\,,

(132)

where ”OS” denotes an evaluation on shell, involving the vanishing of the constraints and use of Hamilton’s equations of motion in the right-hand side for the time-derivatives,
\dot{q}_{ab}=\{q_{ab},{\cal H}\}
.
The gauge parameters
(\epsilon^{\bar{0}},\epsilon^{a})
in (
132
) are related to the diffeomorphism-generator vector field
\xi^{\mu}
by the following change of basis,

\displaystyle\xi^{\mu}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\epsilon^{\bar{0}}n^{\mu}+\epsilon^{a}s^{\mu}_{a}=\xi^{t}t^{\mu}+\xi^{a}s^{\mu}_{a}\,,

\displaystyle\xi^{t}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{N}\quad,\quad\xi^{a}=\epsilon^{a}-\frac{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{N}N^{a}\,.

(133)

On the other hand, the canonical decomposition of spacetime diffeomorphisms and proper Lorentz transformations—the latter denoted by
\delta^{\rm SL(2,\mathbb{C})}
in what follows—of the non-dynamical components of the connection are respectively given by

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}K_{t}^{i}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\delta^{\rm SL(2,\mathbb{C})}_{\xi^{\mu}\Gamma_{\mu},\xi^{\mu}K_{\mu}}K_{t}^{i}+\epsilon^{a}N^{b}F_{ab}^{0i}

(134)

\displaystyle\!\!+\left(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}N^{a}-N\epsilon^{a}\right)F_{0a}^{0i}\,,

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\Gamma_{t}^{i}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\delta^{\rm SL(2,\mathbb{C})}_{\xi^{\mu}\Gamma_{\mu},\xi^{\mu}K_{\mu}}\Gamma_{t}^{i}+\epsilon^{a}N^{b}{\cal F}_{ab}^{i}

(135)

\displaystyle\!\!+\left(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}N^{a}-N\epsilon^{a}\right){\cal F}_{0a}^{i}\,,

and

\displaystyle\delta^{\rm SL(2,\mathbb{C})}_{\theta,\beta}K_{t}^{i}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\dot{\beta}^{i}+\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}\left(\beta^{j}\Gamma_{t}^{k}+\theta^{j}K_{t}^{k}\right)\,,

(136)

\displaystyle\delta^{\rm SL(2,\mathbb{C})}_{\theta,\beta}\Gamma_{t}^{i}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\dot{\theta}^{i}+\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}\left(\theta^{j}\Gamma_{t}^{k}-\beta^{j}K_{t}^{k}\right)\,.

(137)

Using this, the canonical gauge transformations (
127
)-(
130
) can be written as linear combinations of diffeomorphisms and Lorentz transformations:

\delta_{\epsilon,\theta,\beta}\cdot\big|_{\rm OS}=\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\cdot+\delta^{\rm SL(2,\mathbb{C})}_{\theta-\xi^{\mu}\Gamma_{\mu},\beta-\xi^{\mu}K_{\mu}}\cdot\big|_{\rm OS}\,.

(138)

All physical fields are therefore expected to follow this transformation under the gauge flow.

In particular, the covariance condition of the spacetime metric (
132
) agrees with the transformation (
138
) if the metric is Lorentz-invariant under the gauge flow.
This is indeed the case because the lapse and shift do not suffer transformations under the flow of the Lorentz–Gauss constraints, as implied by (
127
) and (
128
), and neither does the spatial metric,

\displaystyle\{q^{ab},G_{i}[\theta^{i}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,,

(139)

\displaystyle\{q^{ab},L_{i}[\beta^{i}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,.

(140)

This, in turn, implies that the vector constraint generates spatial diffeomorphisms of the spatial metric on shell:

\displaystyle\{q^{ab}(x),H_{c}[\epsilon^{c}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\mathcal{L}_{\vec{\epsilon}}\,q^{ab}-L_{i}\left[\{q^{ab}(x),K_{b}^{i}N^{b}\}\right]

(141)

\displaystyle\!\!-G_{i}\left[\{q^{ab}(x),\Gamma_{b}^{i}N^{b}\}\right]\,.

Finally, the canonical gauge transformation of the spatial metric does not contain derivatives of the normal gauge function,

\frac{\partial\{q^{ab},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{c_{1}}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\bigg|_{\rm OS}=\frac{\partial\{q^{ab},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{c_{1}}\partial_{c_{2}}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\bigg|_{\rm OS}=\dotsi=0\,.

(142)

This is a necessary condition—not implied by the constraint algebra—for (
132
) to hold
EMGCov
.
Using equations (
127
), (
128
), and (
139
)-(
142
), we confirm that the covariance condition (
132
) is indeed satisfied.

Using (
127
), (
128
), and the fact that the Lorentz, Gauss, and vector constraints generate a linear combination of Lorentz transformations and spatial diffeomorphisms, the covariance condition of the tetrad

\delta_{\epsilon,\theta,\beta}e_{\mu}^{I}\big|_{\rm OS}=\mathcal{L}_{\xi}e_{\mu}^{I}+\delta^{\rm SL(2,\mathbb{C})}_{\theta-\xi^{\mu}\Gamma_{\mu},\beta-\xi^{\mu}K_{\mu}}e_{\mu}^{I}\big|_{\rm OS}\,,

(143)

can be reduced to a set of conditions for the Hamiltonian constraint, given by

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\{v_{i},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{c}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\bigg|_{\rm O.S.}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\gamma\sqrt{8\pi G}}{\cal P}^{c}_{i}\bigg|_{\rm OS}\,,

(144)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial^{2}\{v_{i},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{c_{1}}\partial_{c_{2}}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\bigg|_{\rm OS}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,,

(145)

and

\displaystyle\!\!\left(\delta^{a}_{c}\delta^{k}_{i}-\frac{1}{2}{\cal P}^{a}_{i}({\cal P}^{-1})^{k}_{c}\right)\frac{\partial\{{\cal P}^{c}_{k},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{d}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\bigg|_{\rm OS}

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad=-\frac{\gamma\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}v^{j}{\cal P}^{a}_{j}{\cal P}^{d}_{i}\bigg|_{\rm OS}\,,

(146)

\displaystyle\!\!\left(\delta^{a}_{c}\delta^{k}_{i}-\frac{1}{2}{\cal P}^{a}_{i}({\cal P}^{-1})^{k}_{c}\right)\frac{\partial^{2}\{{\cal P}^{c}_{k},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{d_{1}}\partial_{d_{2}}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\bigg|_{\rm OS}=0\,.\qquad

(147)

Similarly, the covariance condition of the connection,

\delta_{\epsilon,\theta,\beta}\mathchoice{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}{J}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}{J}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}{J}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}{J}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$} OS}=\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathchoice{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}{J}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}{J}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}{J}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}{J}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebo OS}\,,

(148)

reduces to

\frac{\partial\{\mathchoice{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}{J}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}{J}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}{J}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}{J}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][ OS}=\frac{\partial\{\mathchoice{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}{J}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}{J}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}{J}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}{J}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{c_{1}}\partial_{c_{2}}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\bigg|_{\rm OS}=\dotsi=0\,,

(149)

that of the fermion field,

\delta_{\epsilon,\theta,\beta}\Psi\big|_{\rm OS}=\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\Psi+\delta^{\rm SL(2,\mathbb{C})}_{\theta-\xi^{\mu}\Gamma_{\mu},\beta-\xi^{\mu}K_{\mu}}\Psi\big|_{\rm OS}\,,

(150)

to

\frac{\partial\{\Psi,H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{c_{1}}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\bigg|_{\rm OS}=\frac{\partial\{\Psi,H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{c_{1}}\partial_{c_{2}}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\bigg|_{\rm OS}=\dotsi=0\,,

(151)

and that of the fermion momentum,

\delta_{\epsilon,\theta,\beta}P_{\Psi}\big|_{\rm OS}=\mathcal{L}_{\xi}P_{\Psi}+\delta^{\rm SL(2,\mathbb{C})}_{\theta-\xi^{\mu}\Gamma_{\mu},\beta-\xi^{\mu}K_{\mu}}P_{\Psi}\big|_{\rm OS}\,,

(152)

to

\frac{\partial\{P_{\Psi}\overline{\Xi^{-1}},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{c_{1}}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\bigg|_{\rm OS}=\frac{\partial\{P_{\Psi}\overline{\Xi^{-1}},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{c_{1}}\partial_{c_{2}}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\bigg|_{\rm OS}=\dotsi=0\,,

(153)

or, using (
142
) and (
144
), to

\frac{\partial\{P_{\Psi},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{c}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\bigg|_{\rm OS}=\frac{8\pi G}{\sqrt{\det q}}{\cal P}^{c}_{j}P_{\Psi}\left[v^{j}-\left(\gamma^{0}-v_{i}\gamma^{i}\right)\gamma^{j}\right]

(154)

and

\frac{\partial\{P_{\Psi},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{c_{1}}\partial_{c_{2}}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\bigg|_{\rm OS}=0\,,

(155)

where we used the fact that
P_{\Psi}\overline{\Xi^{-1}}
constitutes a co-spinor field to arrive at (
153
).
The covariance conditions of
\overline{\Psi}
and
\overline{P_{\Psi}}
are given by the complex conjugate of those of
\Psi
and
P_{\Psi}
because all the constraints are Hermitian upon use of the reality conditions and hence do not constitute independent covariance conditions.

The connection and fermion covariance conditions (
149
) and (
151
) can be readily verified to hold because the Hamiltonian constraint has no derivatives of their momenta; similarly, the covariance conditions for the fermion momentum (
154
) and (
155
) can be easily shown to hold from the simple dependence of the
H
on derivatives of
\Psi
.
On the other hand, proving that the covariance conditions of the tetrad (
144
)-(
147
) hold requires several detailed computations, but they indeed hold in the vacuum
Tetrads
and the presence of fermions does not imply contributions to these equations because the respective contributions to the constraints do not contain derivatives of the configuration variables conjugate to
v_{i}
or
{\cal P}^{a}_{i}
.

All physical fields correspond to geometric objects, matter currents, or stress-energy tensor fields that are constructed with the tetrad, the connection, and
\Psi
; therefore, the canonical gauge transformations of all physical fields correspond to linear combinations of spacetime diffeomorphisms and
{\rm SL}(2,\mathbb{C})
transformations.
We conclude that our canonical system is indeed covariant in the extended phase space.
This property holds on the second-class constraint surface, which we show in the next section by use of the corresponding Dirac brackets.

IV.4
Dirac observables

The functionals

\displaystyle\mathfrak{L}_{i}[\alpha^{i}]\!\!

P}^{a}_{l}\right)\,,

\{\mathfrak{G}_{i},G_{j}[\Gamma_{t}^{j}]\}|_{\rm OS}=-\partial_{a}\left(\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{l}}}_{{{i}{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{l}}}_{{{i}{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{l}}}_{{{i}{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$ as well as

K}^{a}_{k}-\Gamma^{j}_{b}\tilde{\cal P}^{a}_{k}\right)v^{q}{\cal P}^{b}_{q}+\frac{2}{\gamma^{2}}{\cal P}^{[a}_{i}{\cal P}^{b]}_{q}\left(K_{b}^{q}-\zeta\Gamma_{b}^{q}\right)

(164)

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad+\left({\cal P}^{a}_{j}iP_{\Psi}\left(\gamma^{0}-v_{m}\gamma^{m}\right)\gamma^{j}S_{0i}\Psi+c.c.\right)\bigg)\Bigg]\,,

\displaystyle\{\mathfrak{G}_{i},H[N]\}|_{\rm OS}\!\!

\displaystyle\!\!\partial_{a}\Bigg[N\frac{\gamma\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}\bigg(\mathfrak{G}_{i}v^{q}{\cal P}^{a}_{l}+\Gamma^{k}_{b}\tilde{\cal K}^{a}_{l}\right)v^{q}{\cal P}^{b}_{q}-\frac{{\cal P}^{[a}_{i}{\cal P}^{b]}_{q}}{\gamma^{2}}\left(\Gamma_{b}^{q}+\zeta K_{b}^{q}\right)

(165)

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad+\left({\cal P}^{a}_{j}P_{\Psi}\left(\gamma^{0}-v_{m}\gamma^{m}\right)\gamma^{j}iS_{i}\Psi+c.c.\right)\bigg)\Bigg]\,.

The boundary terms imply conserved densitized currents: Defining

\mathcal{L}_{i}^{t}\equiv\mathfrak{L}_{i}\quad,\quad\{\mathfrak{L}_{i},{\cal H}\}|_{\rm OS}=:-\partial_{a}\mathfrak{L}_{i}^{a}\,,

(166)

and

\mathcal{G}_{i}^{t}\equiv\mathfrak{G}_{i}\quad,\quad\{\mathfrak{G}_{i},{\cal H}\}|_{\rm OS}=:-\partial_{a}\mathfrak{G}_{i}^{a}\,,

(167)

the densitized four-currents
{\cal L}_{i}^{\mu}=({\cal L}_{i}^{t},{\cal L}_{i}^{a})
and
{\cal G}_{i}^{\mu}=({\cal G}_{i}^{t},{\cal G}_{i}^{a})
are conserved on shell by definition:
\dot{\cal L}_{i}^{t}=\{{\cal L}_{i},{\cal H}\}|_{\rm OS}=-\partial_{a}{\cal L}_{i}^{a}
and
\dot{\cal G}_{i}^{t}=\{{\cal G}_{i},{\cal H}\}|_{\rm OS}=-\partial_{a}{\cal G}_{i}^{a}
, and hence
\partial_{\mu}{\cal L}_{i}^{\mu}=0
and
\partial_{\mu}{\cal G}_{i}^{\mu}=0
.

Moreover, these observables form a local Lorentz algebra,

\displaystyle\{\mathfrak{G}_{i}(x),\mathfrak{G}_{j}(y)\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scri (168)

\displaystyle\{\mathfrak{G}_{i}(x),\mathfrak{L}_{j}(y)\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scri (169)

\displaystyle\{\mathfrak{L}_{i}(x),\mathfrak{L}_{j}(y)\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathfrak{G}_{k}\delta^{3}(x-y)\,.

(170)

The appearance of the fermionic variables in the observables (
156
) and (
157
) clarifies their physical meaning: They are spin charges associated to the conserved spin currents
\mathcal{L}_{i}^{\mu}
and
\mathcal{G}_{i}^{\mu}
.

Similarly, we expect that the densitized electric current
J^{\mu}=|e|e^{\mu}_{I}\overline{\Psi}\gamma^{I}\Psi
be conserved too.
Indeed, the phase-space functional

{\cal Q}[\alpha]=-i\int{\rm d}^{3}x\alpha\left(P_{\Psi}\Psi-\overline{\Psi}\,\overline{P_{\Psi}}\right)\,,

(171)

whose local version is equivalent to
J^{t}
if the relation (
47
) is used, commutes with the first-class constraints up to boundary terms:

\{{\cal Q},L_{j}[K_{t}^{j}]\}=0\,,

(172)

\{{\cal Q},G_{j}[\Gamma_{t}^{j}]\}=0\,,

(173)

\{{\cal Q},H_{a}[N^{a}]\}=\partial_{a}\left(\mathcal{Q}N^{a}\right)\,,

(174)

and

\displaystyle\{{\cal Q},H[N]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\partial_{a}\left[N\frac{\gamma\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}{\cal P}^{a}_{j}\left(\mathcal{Q}v^{j}+iP_{\Psi}\left(\gamma^{0}-v_{m}\gamma^{m}\right)\gamma^{j}\Psi-i\overline{\Psi}\gamma^{j}\left(\gamma^{0}-v_{m}\gamma^{m}\right)\overline{P_{\Psi}}\right)\right]\,.

(175)

The boundary terms can be identified with the spatial components of the electric current
J^{a}
such that

\dot{\cal Q}=-\partial_{a}J^{a}

(176)

or
\partial_{\mu}J^{\mu}=0
on shell.

The bracket of this observable with itself forms a local Abelian algebra,

\{{\cal Q}(x),{\cal Q}(y)\}=0\,.

(177)

IV.5
Fermion dynamics in the extended phase space

Hamilton’s equations of motion for the fermionic variables result in

\dot{\Psi}=\{\Psi,{\cal H}\}=\Theta^{a}\partial_{a}\Psi+i\Omega\Psi\,,

(178)

and

\dot{P}_{\Psi}=\{P_{\Psi},{\cal H}\}=\partial_{a}\left(P_{\Psi}\Theta^{a}\right)-iP_{\Psi}\Omega\,.

(179)

A direct inspection shows that (
178
) is equivalent to (
61
).

Furthermore, using (
64
), the equation of motion of the momentum (
179
) can be written as

\displaystyle\dot{\Phi}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\Theta^{a}\partial_{a}\Phi+i\Omega\Phi

(180)

\displaystyle\!\!-\Xi^{-1}\left[\{\Xi,{\cal H}\}-\partial_{a}\left(\overline{\Theta^{a}}\Xi\right)-i\left(\overline{\Omega}\Xi-\Xi\Omega\right)\right]\Phi\,,

upon complex conjugation, where the last line is proportional to the covariant divergence of the tetrad (
77
) and hence (
180
)
is identical to the equation of motion (
62
).
This confirms that the canonical system, off the Dirac-fermion constraint surface, indeed describes the dynamics of the extended fermion action (
60
).

For completeness, we compute

\displaystyle\!\!\{\Xi,{\cal H}\}-\partial_{a}\left(\overline{\Theta^{a}}\Xi\right)-i\left(\overline{\Omega}\Xi-\Xi\Omega\right)|_{\rm OS}

(181)

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad=\frac{3i}{8}\frac{8\pi G\gamma N}{1+\zeta^{2}}\left(1-i\alpha\gamma^{5}\right)\Bigg[P_{\Psi}\left(\gamma^{0}-v_{m}\gamma^{m}\right)\gamma^{0}\left(1+\zeta i\gamma^{5}\right)\Psi\gamma^{0}-P_{\Psi}\left(\gamma^{0}-v_{s}\gamma^{s}\right)\gamma_{i}\left(1+\zeta i\gamma^{5}\right)\Psi\gamma^{i}

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad-\frac{2}{3}\delta_{i[p}\mathfrak{K}^{p}_{l]}P_{\Psi}\left(\gamma^{0}-v_{s}\gamma^{s}\right)\gamma^{l}\left(i\gamma^{5}-\zeta\right)\Psi\gamma^{i}+c.c.\Bigg]

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad=\frac{3i}{16}\frac{\sqrt{\det q}}{\gamma}\frac{8\pi GN}{1+\zeta^{2}}\left(1-i\alpha\gamma^{5}\right)\Bigg[-\overline{\Phi}\gamma^{0}\left[\left(\zeta-\alpha\right)\gamma^{5}-i\left(1+\alpha\zeta\right)\right]\Psi\gamma^{0}+\overline{\Phi}\gamma_{i}\left[\left(\zeta-\alpha\right)\gamma^{5}-i\left(1+\alpha\zeta\right)\right]\Psi\gamma^{i}

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{2}{3}\delta_{i[p}\mathfrak{K}^{p}_{l]}\overline{\Phi}\gamma^{l}\left(\left(1+\zeta\alpha\right)\gamma^{5}-i\left(\alpha-\zeta\right)\right)\Psi\gamma^{i}+c.c\Bigg]\,,

where
{\rm OS}
here refers to an evaluation on the first-class constraint surface and we used (
64
) to get the second equality—see App.
A.1
for intermediate steps of this calculation.

It follows that the Dirac-fermion constraint (
59
)—and hence also (
58
)—is second-class for non-trivial
\zeta
,
\alpha
, or
\mathfrak{K}_{ij}
:

\displaystyle\{C_{\Psi},{\cal H}\}|_{\rm OS}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\left[\{\Xi,{\cal H}\}-\partial_{a}\left(\overline{\Theta^{a}}\Xi\right)-i\left(\overline{\Omega}\Xi-\Xi\Omega\right)\right]\Psi

(182)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{3i}{8}\frac{\sqrt{\det q}}{\gamma}\frac{8\pi GN}{1+\zeta^{2}}\left(1-i\alpha\gamma^{5}\right)\left[-\overline{\Psi}\gamma^{0}\left(\zeta-\alpha\right)\gamma^{5}\Psi\gamma^{0}+\overline{\Psi}\gamma^{l}\left(\delta_{li}\left(\zeta-\alpha\right)+\frac{2}{3}\delta_{i[p}\mathfrak{K}^{p}_{l]}\left(1+\zeta\alpha\right)\right)\gamma^{5}\Psi\gamma^{i}\right]\Psi\,,

where we set
C_{\Psi}=0=C_{\overline{\Psi}}
after the evaluation of the brackets.

V
Second-class constraints

V.1
Gravitational second-class constraints

The fermionic contributions to the Lorentz, Gauss, and vector constraints do not involve the components of the connection in a way that they can contribute to the gravitational secondary second-class constraint (
54
) on shell, while their gravitational contributions similarly vanish on shell upon evaluation of the primary second-class constraint (
53
)
Tetrads
.
Therefore, the only nontrivial contribution comes from the second term of the Hamiltonian constraint,

\frac{\delta H^{(2)}[N]}{\delta{\cal B}^{ij}}=N\frac{\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\gamma\sqrt{8\pi G}}{\cal T}_{ij}\,,

where
{\cal T}_{ij}
is given by the torsion term (
80
).
This expression can be written as

(188)

We can substitute the solution to the second-class constraints,
{\cal B}_{ij}=\bar{\cal B}_{ij}
and
\mathfrak{K}_{ij}=0
, in the first-class constraints and the symplectic structure, thereby reducing the phase space, now spanned by the canonical pairs
({\cal P},{\cal D})
,
(v,{\cal E})
,
(\Psi,P_{\Psi})
, and
(\overline{\Psi},\overline{P_{\Psi}})
.
However, the constraints turn out to have a complicated dependence on the reduced phase space, which complicates explicit computations of their brackets.
This complication is bypassed by preserving the extended phase space and use the corresponding Dirac brackets.

V.2
Dirac brackets

The Poisson brackets between the second-class constraints in the extended phase space are given by

\{\mathfrak{K}_{ij}(x),\mathfrak{K}^{kl}(y)\}=0\,,

(189)

\{\mathfrak{K}_{ij}(x),{\cal
